scripts/dev-harness/spikes/barconfig-window/RESULT-2026-11-27.txt

D-HOST-15, the barConfig window on the host's own order, in the nested
cage: lane-dh15m, 2026-11-27 (09:22 to 10:50 CST), branch dh15 from
27e2a84. The reading is docs/QA-RESULTS.md "D-HOST-15: the barConfig window
on the host's own order (lane-dh15m, 2026-11-27)".

THE RUNS. Each under the heavy lock with `flock -o` and nice 10, in one
nested headless cage per run on ../../private-bus.sh, every quickshell
under gdb, through a lane wrapper that printed the `# run`, `# start`,
`# env`, `# arms` and `# end` lines:
  RIGW_DEV=27e2a84 RIGW_KEEP=1 <env> run.sh ARM...
"bw" in a name is RIGW_BAR=1 RIGW_WIDGETS=1 (the host's bar and its 19
first-party bar widgets in the root: the host's order); "rr" neither (the
reload rig's roots). The plugin is 27e2a84's ALLOWLIST export (export.py,
commit d0ba9ddd), unchanged but for service-log.patch on the decoy copy (not
in NP-bw). Host: omarchy 4.5.0-3 (shell.qml and services/PluginRegistry.qml
pinned by ../codebehind-reload/make-root.py), quickshell 0.3.2.r7.g2b4501a-1,
qt6-base 6.12.0-3, cage 0.3.1-1. Crash journal 89 before and after every
shell of every run; gdb reported no signal.

L-rr, C-rr-warm and P-rr ran under 8 leftover busy-loop burners (the first
run.sh's stop used a needle qa_signal_named does not match: an argv item
must START with it), and so did the first two I-bw starts (10:30:29 and
10:30:56); the lane stopped the eight recorded pids at 10:31:14. L-rr-2,
C-rr-warm-2 and P-rr-2 are the clean reruns, with the fix.

WHAT IS PRINTED, AND HOW IT WAS FILTERED. For each run: the lines run.sh and
the wrapper printed outside the starts, as written; and for each start, its
`==` line and then analyze.py's block, as committed, RE-READ over the files
the start kept (qs.log, steps.log, snaps.jsonl, rstate.jsonl, shell.json and
state.json at the start and the end). The runs printed the same blocks at
the time, from the analyzer of the moment: before 10:36 it had no "at the
first syncPluginApis" line and compared the sources with the start folder's
play.m3u (so a re-read elsewhere reads the fixture's playlist off
shell.json.start instead); nothing else differs. The work folder's path is
written <work>, the lane's scratch <scratch>, the worktree <worktree>, and
a character outside ASCII (the plugin's notification icon, a Nerd Font
code point, in the stand-in log's lines) as <U+XXXX>, because the gate's
ASCII scan covers this folder.
Nothing is dropped: run.sh prints none of the shell's own log lines (the
cage's warnings the reload rig's RESULT files drop stay in each start's
kept qs.log).

========================================================================
